  <!-- Creator Profile JS -->
  <script>
    let currentProfile = {};

    async function loadProfile() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/profile');
        const data = await res.json();
        currentProfile = data;

        selectPill('ppContentType', data.content_type || 'gaming');
        selectPill('ppAudienceAge', data.audience_age || '13-25');
        selectPill('ppStyle', data.style || 'energetic');
        document.getElementById('ppClipStyle').value = data.clip_style || 'start with action not buildup';
        document.getElementById('ppAvoid').value = data.avoid || 'slow sections, long talking, boring intros';

        const wm = data.watermark || {};
        document.getElementById('wmEnabled').checked = !!wm.enabled;
        document.getElementById('wmText').value = wm.text || '@yourchannel';
        document.getElementById('wmOpacity').value = (wm.opacity !== undefined) ? wm.opacity * 100 : 80;
        document.getElementById('wmOpacityVal').textContent = Math.round((wm.opacity !== undefined) ? wm.opacity * 100 : 80) + '%';
        document.getElementById('wmSize').value = wm.size || 36;

        const isImage = !!wm.image_path;
        selectPill('wmType', isImage ? 'image' : 'text');
        wmTypeChange(isImage ? 'image' : 'text');

        initWatermarkPosGrid(wm.position || 'bottom-right');

        wmToggle();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load profile:', err);
      }
    }

    function selectPill(rowId, val) {
      const row = document.getElementById(rowId);
      if (!row) return;
      const btns = row.getElementsByTagName('button');
      for (const btn of btns) {
        const onclick = btn.getAttribute('onclick') || '';
        const matches = onclick.match(/'([^']+)'/g);
        if (matches && matches.length >= 2) {
          const btnVal = matches[1].replace(/'/g, '');
          if (btnVal === val) {
            for (const b of btns) b.classList.remove('active');
            btn.classList.add('active');
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    function ppPick(rowId, val, btn) {
      const row = document.getElementById(rowId);
      const btns = row.getElementsByTagName('button');
      for (const b of btns) b.classList.remove('active');
      btn.classList.add('active');
    }

    function wmToggle() {
      const enabled = document.getElementById('wmEnabled').checked;
      document.getElementById('wmSettings').style.display = enabled ? 'block' : 'none';
    }

    function wmTypeChange(type) {
      document.getElementById('wmTextInputProfile').style.display = type === 'text' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('wmImageInput').style.display = type === 'image' ? 'block' : 'none';
    }

    const posGridList = [
      'top-left', 'top-center', 'top-right',
      'center-left', 'center', 'center-right',
      'bottom-left', 'bottom-center', 'bottom-right'
    ];

    function initWatermarkPosGrid(activePos) {
      const grid = document.getElementById('wmPosGrid');
      grid.innerHTML = posGridList.map(pos => {
        const isActive = pos === activePos;
        const arrow = getArrow(pos);
        return `<button class="clip-pill ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="ppPick('wmPosGrid','${pos}',this)" title="${pos}">${arrow}</button>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    function getArrow(pos) {
      const map = {
        'top-left': '↖', 'top-center': '↑', 'top-right': '↗',
        'center-left': '←', 'center': '•', 'center-right': '→',
        'bottom-left': '↙', 'bottom-center': '↓', 'bottom-right': '↘'
      };
      return map[pos] || '•';
    }

    function toggleProfilePanel() {
      const overlay = document.getElementById('profileOverlay');
      const panel = document.getElementById('profilePanel');
      const isOpen = panel.classList.contains('open');
      if (isOpen) {
        overlay.classList.remove('open');
        panel.classList.remove('open');
      } else {
        loadProfile();
        overlay.classList.add('open');
        panel.classList.add('open');
      }
    }

    async function saveProfile() {
      const contentType = getActivePillVal('ppContentType');
      const audienceAge = getActivePillVal('ppAudienceAge');
      const style = getActivePillVal('ppStyle');
      const clipStyle = document.getElementById('ppClipStyle').value;
      const avoid = document.getElementById('ppAvoid').value;

      const wmEnabled = document.getElementById('wmEnabled').checked;
      const wmType = getActivePillVal('wmType');
      const wmText = document.getElementById('wmText').value;
      const wmOpacity = parseFloat(document.getElementById('wmOpacity').value) / 100;
      const wmSize = parseInt(document.getElementById('wmSize').value) || 36;
      const wmPosition = getActivePillVal('wmPosGrid') || 'bottom-right';

      const profile = {
        name: 'Creator',
        content_type: contentType,
        audience_age: audienceAge,
        style: style,
        clip_style: clipStyle,
        avoid: avoid,
        language: 'auto',
        example_hooks: currentProfile.example_hooks || ["You won't believe what happened next...", "This is insane...", "Wait for it..."],
        watermark: {
          enabled: wmEnabled,
          text: wmText,
          image_path: currentProfile.watermark?.image_path || '',
          position: wmPosition,
          opacity: wmOpacity,
          size: wmSize
        }
      };

      const imageFileInp = document.getElementById('wmImageFile');
      if (wmEnabled && wmType === 'image' && imageFileInp && imageFileInp.files[0]) {
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('watermark', imageFileInp.files[0]);
        try {
          const res = await fetch('/api/profile/watermark-image', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success && data.path) {
            profile.watermark.image_path = data.path;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to upload watermark image:', err);
          toast('Failed to upload watermark image');
          return;
        }
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/profile', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(profile)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
          toast('Profile saved successfully');
          toggleProfilePanel();
        } else {
          toast('Failed to save profile');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to save profile:', err);
        toast('Error saving profile');
      }
    }

    function getActivePillVal(rowId) {
      const row = document.getElementById(rowId);
      if (!row) return '';
      const activeBtn = row.querySelector('button.active');
      if (!activeBtn) return '';
      const onclick = activeBtn.getAttribute('onclick');
      const m = onclick.match(/'([^']+)'/g);
      if (m && m.length >= 2) {
        return m[1].replace(/'/g, '');
      }
      return '';
    }
  </script>

  <script>
    document.getElementById('themeToggle')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const next = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
      localStorage.setItem('theme', next);
    });
  </script>
  </body>

</html>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: backend\src\captionEngine.js

/**
 * Caption Engine v4 — Gemini Words + ElevenLabs Scribe Safety Net
 *
 * Transcription pipeline:
 *   1. Use word-level timestamps from the full-video Gemini transcript (primary)
 *   2. If a clip has no Gemini words (gap in transcript) → ElevenLabs Scribe v2 (safety net)
 *   3. If no word timestamps available → speech-rate heuristic (last resort)
 *
 * OpenAI Whisper dependency has been removed entirely.
 */
import { buildStyleConfig, styleToFfmpegForceStyle } from './captionStyles.js';
import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import dotenv from 'dotenv';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);
const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
dotenv.config({ path: path.join(__dirname, '../.env') });

// Words per caption chunk — keep it short for social media readability
const WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 3;
const EMPHASIS_WORDS = new Set([
  'NO', 'BRO', 'WAIT', 'RUN', 'WHAT', 'INSANE', 'WTF', 'OMG', "LET'S GO", 'HEADSHOT', 'WIN', 'LOSE', 'STOP', 'OH', 'HOLY', 'LMAO', 'CLUTCH', 'RAGE'
]);
const EMOTION_HINTS = {
  scream: ['scream', 'screaming', 'ahhh', 'aaa', 'what', 'nooo', 'omg', 'bro', 'wait'],
  laugh: ['haha', 'lol', 'lmao', 'laugh', 'funny', 'crazy'],
  surprise: ['wait', 'what', 'insane', 'holy', 'no way', 'wtf'],
  excitement: ['let\'s go', 'win', 'clutch', 'headshot', 'yes', 'go', 'ohhh'],
  fear: ['no', 'run', 'dont', 'stop', 'help'],
  anger: ['rage', 'annoying', 'trash', 'lose', 'bad']
};
const EMOJI_POOL = ['💀', '😂', '🔥', '😭', '😳', '🤯'];

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Time formatter for SRT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function formatAssTime(sec) {
  const s = Math.max(0, sec);
  let h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  let m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  let ss = Math.floor(s % 60);
  let cs = Math.floor((s % 1) * 100);
  return `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(ss).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(cs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function formatSrtTime(sec) {
  const s = Math.max(0, sec);
  let h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  let m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  let ss = Math.floor(s % 60);
  let ms = Math.round((s % 1) * 1000);
  if (ms >= 1000) {
    ms -= 1000;
    ss += 1;
    if (ss >= 60) { ss -= 60; m += 1; }
    if (m >= 60) { m -= 60; h += 1; }
  }
  return `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(ss).padStart(2, '0')},${String(ms).padStart(3, '0')}`;
}

function normalizeWord(text = '') {
  return String(text || '').trim().replace(/^[^A-Za-z0-9]+|[^A-Za-z0-9]+$/g, '').toUpperCase();
}

function detectEmotionHint(text = '') {
  const normalized = String(text || '').toLowerCase();
  for (const [emotion, hints] of Object.entries(EMOTION_HINTS)) {
    if (hints.some(hint => normalized.includes(hint))) return emotion;
  }
  return null;
}

function pickWordStyle(word, styleConfig = {}) {
  const normalized = normalizeWord(word);
  const emphasisMap = styleConfig.emphasis || {};
  if (EMPHASIS_WORDS.has(normalized)) {
    return { color: emphasisMap.important || '#FACC15', weight: 'bold', emphasis: 'important' };
  }
  if (normalized === 'WHAT' || normalized === 'WAIT' || normalized === 'BRO' || normalized === 'OMG' || normalized === 'WTF') {
    return { color: emphasisMap.question || '#60A5FA', weight: 'bold', emphasis: 'question' };
  }
  if (normalized === 'WIN' || normalized === 'LET\'S GO' || normalized === 'CLUTCH') {
    return { color: emphasisMap.positive || '#4ADE80', weight: 'bold', emphasis: 'positive' };
  }
  if (normalized === 'LOSE' || normalized === 'NO' || normalized === 'RUN') {
    return { color: emphasisMap.danger || '#F87171', weight: 'bold', emphasis: 'danger' };
  }
  return { color: emphasisMap.default || '#FFFFFF', weight: 'normal', emphasis: 'default' };
}

function maybeAddEmoji(text, styleConfig = {}, cueIndex = 0) {
  if (!styleConfig.emoji) return text;
  const shouldAdd = cueIndex % 7 === 0 && Math.random() > 0.4;
  if (!shouldAdd) return text;
  return `${text} ${EMOJI_POOL[Math.floor(Math.random() * EMOJI_POOL.length)]}`;
}

function buildCueText(words = [], styleConfig = {}, cueIndex = 0) {
  const parts = words.map((word) => {
    const text = String(word.word || '').trim();
    if (!text) return '';
    const style = pickWordStyle(text, styleConfig);
    return { text, style };
  }).filter(Boolean);

  if (!parts.length) return '';
  const first = parts[0];
  const second = parts[Math.min(1, parts.length - 1)] || null;
  const hasStrongEmphasis = parts.some(part => part.style.emphasis !== 'default');
  const displayText = parts.map(part => part.text).join(' ');
  const maybeEmojied = hasStrongEmphasis ? maybeAddEmoji(displayText, styleConfig, cueIndex) : displayText;
  return maybeEmojied;
}

function buildCueTiming(words = [], clipDuration, cueIndex = 0) {
  if (!words || !words.length) return { start: 0, end: Math.min(clipDuration, 0.8) };
  const start = Math.max(0, Number(words[0].start || 0));
  let end = Number(words[words.length - 1].end || start + 0.8);
  const gapBefore = words.length > 1 ? Math.max(0, Number(words[1].start || end) - Number(words[0].end || start)) : 0;
  const phrasePadding = cueIndex % 3 === 0 ? 0.06 : 0.03;
  const minDuration = cueIndex % 2 === 0 ? 0.65 : 0.5;
  if (end - start < minDuration) end = start + minDuration;
  if (gapBefore > 0.2) end = Math.max(end, start + 0.85);
  return { start: Math.round(start * 1000) / 1000, end: Math.min(clipDuration, Math.round((end + phrasePadding) * 1000) / 1000) };
}

function splitIntoNaturalLines(text = '', maxChars = 24) {
  const cleaned = String(text || '').trim();
  if (!cleaned) return [cleaned];
  if (cleaned.length <= maxChars) return [cleaned];
  const words = cleaned.split(/\s+/);
  const firstLineWords = [];
  let firstLineLength = 0;
  for (const word of words) {
    if (firstLineLength + word.length + (firstLineWords.length ? 1 : 0) > maxChars) break;
    firstLineWords.push(word);
    firstLineLength += word.length + (firstLineWords.length > 1 ? 1 : 0);
  }
  const remainder = words.slice(firstLineWords.length).join(' ');
  if (!remainder) return [cleaned];
  return [firstLineWords.join(' '), remainder];
}

function getCueSafeAreaMargin(cue = {}, styleConfig = {}) {
  const baseMargin = Number(styleConfig.safeArea?.marginV || styleConfig.marginV || 150);
  const textLength = String(cue.displayText || cue.text || '').length;
  const lineCount = Array.isArray(cue.lines) ? cue.lines.length : 1;
  const isEmotional = ['scream', 'surprise', 'excitement', 'anger'].includes(String(cue.emotion || '').toLowerCase());
  let marginV = baseMargin;
  if (textLength > 24) marginV -= 10;
  if (lineCount > 1) marginV -= 8;
  if (isEmotional) marginV -= 12;
  return Math.max(90, Math.min(180, Math.round(marginV)));
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  ElevenLabs Scribe v2 — safety net for clips
//  where the Gemini transcript has a gap
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
async function extractClipSegment(videoPath, start, duration, outPath) {
  // Re-encode audio for accurate seeking and clean Scribe input.
  // -ss AFTER -i = frame-accurate seek (not keyframe-based).
  // Re-encoding avoids audio glitches from cutting mid-GOP with stream-copy.
  const cmd = [
    'ffmpeg', '-y',
    '-i', `"${videoPath}"`,
    '-ss', String(Math.max(0, start)),
    '-t', String(duration),
    '-vn',
    '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '128k', '-ac', '1', '-ar', '44100',
    '-movflags', '+faststart',
    `"${outPath}"`
  ].join(' ');
  await execAsync(cmd, { maxBuffer: 50 * 1024 * 1024 });
}

async function transcribeWithScribe(videoPath, clipStart, clipDuration, logFn = console.log) {
  const apiKey = process.env.ELEVENLABS_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) {
    logFn('[scribe] No ELEVENLABS_API_KEY — skipping Scribe transcription');
    return null;
  }

  const clipPath = videoPath.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, `_scribe_clip.mp4`);

  try {
    logFn(`[scribe] Extracting clip segment (${clipStart.toFixed(1)}s, ${clipDuration.toFixed(1)}s)...`);
    await extractClipSegment(videoPath, clipStart, clipDuration, clipPath);

    // Dynamic import — the SDK is only loaded when actually needed
    const { ElevenLabsClient } = await import('@elevenlabs/elevenlabs-js');
    const { createReadStream } = await import('fs');

    const client = new ElevenLabsClient({ apiKey });

    logFn('[scribe] Sending clip to ElevenLabs Scribe v2...');
    const result = await client.speechToText.convert({
      file: createReadStream(clipPath),
      modelId: 'scribe_v2',
      timestampsGranularity: 'word',
    });

    if (!result.words || !result.words.length) {
      logFn('[scribe] Scribe returned 0 words');
      return null;
    }

    logFn(`[scribe] Scribe transcribed ${result.words.length} words`);

    // Map Scribe word format { text, start, end, type, speaker_id }
    // into the Gemini word format { word, t } that buildCuesFromWords expects.
    // Scribe's timestamps are relative to the clip (start=0), so we add clipStart
    // to get absolute video time — matching what Gemini provides.
    return result.words
      .filter(w => w.type === 'word' && w.text?.trim())
      .map(w => ({
        word: w.text.trim(),
        t: clipStart + parseFloat(w.start),
      }));

  } catch (err) {
    logFn(`[scribe] Scribe transcription failed: ${err.message}`);
    return null;
  } finally {
    try { if (fs.existsSync(clipPath)) fs.unlinkSync(clipPath); } catch {}
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Run Python stable-ts aligner on extracted audio
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function runStableAlignment(videoPath, clipStart, clipDuration, words, language = 'auto', logFn = console.log) {
  const clipAudioPath = videoPath.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, `_align_clip_${Date.now()}.m4a`);
  const transcriptPath = videoPath.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, `_align_transcript_${Date.now()}.json`);
  const outputPath = videoPath.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, `_align_output_${Date.now()}.json`);
  
  try {
    logFn(`[align] Extracting audio for alignment (${clipStart.toFixed(1)}s, ${clipDuration.toFixed(1)}s)...`);
    await extractClipSegment(videoPath, clipStart, clipDuration, clipAudioPath);
    
    fs.writeFileSync(transcriptPath, JSON.stringify(words, null, 2), 'utf-8');
    
    const alignerScript = path.join(__dirname, 'aligner.py');
    const cmd = `python "${alignerScript}" --audio "${clipAudioPath}" --transcript "${transcriptPath}" --language "${language}" --output "${outputPath}"`;
    
    logFn('[align] Running stable-ts alignment...');
    await execAsync(cmd, { maxBuffer: 10 * 1024 * 1024 });
    
    if (fs.existsSync(outputPath)) {
      const result = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(outputPath, 'utf-8'));
      if (result && result.words && result.words.length > 0) {
        logFn(`[align] Alignment successful! Found ${result.words.length} words.`);
        return result.words;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logFn(`[align] Alignment failed: ${err.message}`);
  } finally {
    try { if (fs.existsSync(clipAudioPath)) fs.unlinkSync(clipAudioPath); } catch {}
    try { if (fs.existsSync(transcriptPath)) fs.unlinkSync(transcriptPath); } catch {}
    try { if (fs.existsSync(outputPath)) fs.unlinkSync(outputPath); } catch {}
  }
  return null;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Phase 2: Semantic Phrase Segmentation Algorithm
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function segmentIntoCards(words, clipDuration, options = {}) {
  if (!words || !words.length) return [];

  // Check if these are already aligned absolute times (if start/end exist).
  // If we came from runStableAlignment, they are relative to 0 of the clip.
  const isAligned = words.some(w => w.start !== undefined && w.end !== undefined);
  
  let clipWords = [];
  if (isAligned) {
    clipWords = words;
  } else {
    // Legacy Gemini fallback (absolute video time -> relative to clip)
    // NOTE: In the fully migrated pipeline, this branch is unused because words are in absolute time.
    clipWords = words
      .map((w, i, arr) => {
        const start = Math.max(0, w.t);
        const nextT = arr[i+1] ? arr[i+1].t : clipDuration;
        const end = Math.min(nextT - 0.05, clipDuration);
        return { word: w.word, start, end };
      });
  }

  if (!clipWords.length) return [];

  const maxWords = options.maxWords || 4;
  const maxChars = options.maxChars || 32;
  const minDisplayTime = 0.55;
  const maxDisplayTime = 2.6;
  const maxReadingSpeed = 20; // chars per second
  const silenceThreshold = 0.24; // 240ms pause forces break

  const cues = [];
  let currentChunk = [];
  let chunkChars = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < clipWords.length; i++) {
    const w = clipWords[i];
    const text = w.word.trim();
    if (!text) continue;

    // 1. Check if there's a silence gap >= 250ms BEFORE this word
    // (except for the first word in the chunk)
    if (currentChunk.length > 0) {
      const prevW = currentChunk[currentChunk.length - 1];
      const silenceBefore = w.start - prevW.end;
      if (silenceBefore >= silenceThreshold) {
        // Flush current card
        flushChunk();
      }
    }

    // 2. Check if adding this word exceeds limits
    if (currentChunk.length > 0 && (currentChunk.length >= maxWords || chunkChars + text.length + 1 > maxChars)) {
      flushChunk();
    }

    currentChunk.push(w);
    chunkChars += text.length + 1;

    // 3. Check if word ends a sentence
    const isPunctuation = /[.!?]$/.test(text);
    if (isPunctuation) {
      flushChunk();
      continue;
    }

    // 4. Punchline detection lookahead
    const nextW = clipWords[i + 1];
    if (nextW) {
      const gapToNext = nextW.start - w.end;
      // If the next gap is a big pause (>500ms), and we have room, don't flush yet
      if (gapToNext >= 0.50 && currentChunk.length <= 2) {
        // Keep accumulating to keep the punchline on one card if possible
        continue;
      }
    } else {
      // Last word
      flushChunk();
    }
  }

  // Ensure any leftover is flushed
  if (currentChunk.length > 0) {
    flushChunk();
  }

  function flushChunk() {
    if (currentChunk.length === 0) return;
    
    let start = currentChunk[0].start;
    let end = currentChunk[currentChunk.length - 1].end;
    
    // Add small trailing buffer (but no more than 100ms)
    end = Math.min(end + 0.1, clipDuration);
    
    // Enforce MIN_DISPLAY_TIME
    if (end - start < minDisplayTime) {
      end = start + minDisplayTime;
    }
    
    // Enforce MAX_DISPLAY_TIME
    if (end - start > maxDisplayTime) {
      end = start + maxDisplayTime;
    }

    const text = currentChunk.map(cw => cw.word).join(' ').trim();
    const cueText = buildCueText(currentChunk, options.styleConfig || {}, cues.length);
    const timing = buildCueTiming(currentChunk, clipDuration, cues.length);
    
    // Check reading speed and extend if too fast
    const duration = timing.end - timing.start;
    if (duration > 0 && (cueText.length / duration) > maxReadingSpeed) {
      const extended = timing.start + (cueText.length / maxReadingSpeed);
      timing.end = Math.min(clipDuration, Math.max(extended, timing.end));
    }
    
    const naturalLines = splitIntoNaturalLines(cueText || text, options.styleConfig?.wordsPerLine ? 22 : 24);
    cues.push({
      start: timing.start,
      end: Math.min(clipDuration, Math.round(timing.end * 1000) / 1000),
      text: text,
      displayText: naturalLines.join('\n'),
      words: currentChunk,
      lines: naturalLines,
      emphasis: cueText !== text ? 'rich' : 'basic',
      emotion: detectEmotionHint(text),
      styleHint: options.styleConfig?.id || 'gaming'
    });
    
    currentChunk = [];
    chunkChars = 0;
  }

  // Post-processing: overlap prevention
  for (let i = 0; i < cues.length - 1; i++) {
    const current = cues[i];
    const next = cues[i + 1];
    
    // If current card overlaps next card
    if (current.end >= next.start) {
      // Force 50ms gap
      current.end = next.start - 0.05;
      if (current.end <= current.start) {
        current.end = current.start + 0.1; // Minimal display
        // Push next card slightly
        next.start = current.end + 0.05;
      }
    }
    current.start = Math.round(current.start * 1000) / 1000;
    current.end = Math.round(current.end * 1000) / 1000;
  }
  
  if (cues.length > 0) {
    const last = cues[cues.length - 1];
    last.start = Math.round(last.start * 1000) / 1000;
    last.end = Math.round(last.end * 1000) / 1000;
  }

  return cues;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Fallback: speech-rate heuristic
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function buildCuesFromTranscript(transcript, clipDuration, options = {}) {
  if (!transcript || !transcript.trim()) return [];

  const words = transcript.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!words.length) return [];

  // Group words into phrases by punctuation and character limits (~25 chars max)
  const phrases = [];
  let currentPhrase = [];
  let currentLength = 0;

  for (const word of words) {
    if (currentPhrase.length > 0 && (currentLength + word.length + 1 > 40 || /^[.!?]$/.test(word))) {
      phrases.push(currentPhrase.join(' '));
      currentPhrase = [];
      currentLength = 0;
    }
    currentPhrase.push(word);
    currentLength += word.length + 1;
    // Break immediately if word ends in punctuation
    if (/[.!?]$/.test(word)) {
      phrases.push(currentPhrase.join(' '));
      currentPhrase = [];
      currentLength = 0;
    }
  }
  if (currentPhrase.length > 0) phrases.push(currentPhrase.join(' '));

  const estimatedSpeechDuration = Math.min(words.length / 2.5, clipDuration * 0.9);
  const speechStart = (clipDuration - estimatedSpeechDuration) * 0.1;
  const secPerWord = estimatedSpeechDuration / words.length;

  const cues = [];
  let wordIndex = 0;

  for (const text of phrases) {
    const chunkWords = text.split(' ').length;
    const start = speechStart + wordIndex * secPerWord;
    const end = Math.min(speechStart + (wordIndex + chunkWords) * secPerWord - 0.05, clipDuration - 0.02);
    
    if (end > start && text.trim()) {
      cues.push({
        start: Math.round(start * 1000) / 1000,
        end: Math.round(end * 1000) / 1000,
        text: text.trim(),
      });
    }
    wordIndex += chunkWords;
  }

  return cues;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Convert cues to SRT string
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function cuesToSRT(cues) {
  if (!cues || !cues.length) return null;
  return cues
    .map((cue, i) => `${i + 1}\n${formatSrtTime(cue.start)} --> ${formatSrtTime(cue.end)}\n${cue.text}`)
    .join('\n\n');
}

export function cuesToASS(cues, styleConfig) {
  if (!cues || !cues.length) return null;
  
  const font = styleConfig.fontFamily || styleConfig.font || 'Montserrat ExtraBold';
  const size = styleConfig.size || 26;
  const primary = styleConfig.primary || '&H00FFFFFF';
  const outline = styleConfig.outline || '&H00000000';
  const back = styleConfig.back || styleConfig.backColour || '&H00000000';
  const safeAreaMarginV = styleConfig.safeArea?.marginV || 150;
  const outlineWidth = styleConfig.outlineWidth || 4;
  const shadowWidth = styleConfig.shadowWidth || 2;
  const animation = styleConfig.animation || 'pop';
  const lineSpacing = styleConfig.lineSpacing || 8;

  // ASS Header
  let ass = `[Script Info]
ScriptType: v4.00+
PlayResX: 1080
PlayResY: 1920
WrapStyle: 1

[V4+ Styles]
Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding
Style: Default,${font},${size},${primary},&H0000FFFF,${outline},${back},-1,0,0,0,100,100,${lineSpacing},0,1,${outlineWidth},${shadowWidth},2,30,30,${safeAreaMarginV},1

[Events]
Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text
`;

  for (const cue of cues) {
    const startAss = formatAssTime(cue.start);
    const endAss = formatAssTime(cue.end);
    const textValue = (cue.displayText || cue.text || '').replace(/\n/g, '\\N');
    const safeText = textValue.replace(/\{/g, '').replace(/\}/g, '');
    const emotionBoost = cue.emotion ? String(cue.emotion).toLowerCase() : '';
    const cueMarginV = getCueSafeAreaMargin(cue, styleConfig);
    let effect = '';

    if (animation === 'bounce') {
      effect = `{\\t(0,120,\\fscx105\\fscy105)}{\\fscx95\\fscy95}`;
    } else if (animation === 'elastic') {
      effect = `{\\t(0,120,\\fscx110\\fscy110)}{\\fscx90\\fscy90}`;
    } else if (animation === 'scale-up') {
      effect = `{\\t(0,120,\\fscx105\\fscy105)}{\\fscx95\\fscy95}`;
    } else if (animation === 'fade') {
      effect = `{\\fad(80,80)}`;
    } else {
      effect = `{\\fscx90\\fscy90\\t(0,100,\\fscx100\\fscy100)}`;
    }

    const intensity = emotionBoost && ['scream', 'surprise', 'excitement', 'anger'].includes(emotionBoost) ? '110' : '100';
    const punch = emotionBoost && ['scream', 'surprise', 'excitement', 'anger'].includes(emotionBoost) ? `{\\fscx${intensity}\\fscy${intensity}}` : '';
    const text = `${effect}${punch}${safeText}`;
    ass += `Dialogue: 0,${startAss},${endAss},Default,,0,0,${cueMarginV},,${text}\n`;
  }
  
  return ass;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  MAIN: Build cues — Gemini words (primary)
//  → ElevenLabs Scribe (gap safety net) → heuristic
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function buildClipCuesWithSTT(clip, videoPath, language = 'auto', logFn = console.log) {
  const duration = clip.end - clip.start;

  // ── PRIORITY 1: Gemini word timestamps + Python stable-ts (Primary Alignment) ──
  if (clip.words && clip.words.length > 2) {
    let alignedWords = await runStableAlignment(videoPath, clip.start, duration, clip.words, language, logFn);
    
    const cues = segmentIntoCards(alignedWords || clip.words, duration, { maxWords: 4, maxChars: 40 });
    if (cues.length > 0) {
      logFn(`[caption] Clip ${clip.index + 1}: Gemini words + alignment → ${cues.length} cues`);
      return cues;
    }
  }

  // ── PRIORITY 2: ElevenLabs Scribe (only for clips with a Gemini gap) ──
  if (process.env.ELEVENLABS_API_KEY) {
    logFn(`[caption] Clip ${clip.index + 1}: no Gemini words — trying ElevenLabs Scribe...`);
    const scribeWords = await transcribeWithScribe(videoPath, clip.start, duration, logFn);
    if (scribeWords && scribeWords.length > 2) {
      const cues = segmentIntoCards(scribeWords, duration, { maxWords: 4, maxChars: 40 });
      if (cues.length > 0) {
        logFn(`[caption] Clip ${clip.index + 1}: Scribe → ${cues.length} cues`);
        return cues;
      }
    }
  }

  // ── PRIORITY 3: Flat transcript heuristic ──
  if (clip.transcript && clip.transcript.trim()) {
    const cues = buildCuesFromTranscript(clip.transcript, duration, { wordsPerChunk: WORDS_PER_CHUNK });
    logFn(`[caption] Clip ${clip.index + 1}: transcript heuristic → ${cues.length} cues`);
    return cues;
  }

  logFn(`[caption] Clip ${clip.index + 1}: no speech found — skipping captions`);
  return [];
}


// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Sync fallback (for backward compat from mediaAnalysis.js)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function buildClipCues(clip, options = {}) {
  const duration = clip.end - clip.start;

  if (clip.words && clip.words.length > 2) {
    const cues = segmentIntoCards(clip.words, duration, { maxWords: 4, maxChars: 40 });
    if (cues.length > 0) return cues;
  }

  const heuristicCues = buildCuesFromTranscript(clip.transcript || '', duration, options);
  if (heuristicCues.length > 0) return heuristicCues;

  // No speech at all — skip captions
  return [];
}

export function getCaptionStyleConfig(style, options = {}, language = 'en') {
  return buildStyleConfig(style, options, language);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Phase 4: Timestamp Shifting (Absolute -> Clip Relative)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function shiftToClipTimeline(cards, clipStart, clipEnd) {
  return cards
    // Keep only cards that overlap with the clip
    .filter(c => c.end > clipStart && c.start < clipEnd)
    // Shift to clip-relative time
    .map(c => ({
      ...c,
      start: Math.max(0, c.start - clipStart),
      end: Math.min(clipEnd - clipStart, c.end - clipStart),
    }))
    // Clamp display times
    .filter(c => c.end > c.start + 0.1);
}

/**
 * Build FFmpeg subtitle filter with Windows-safe path escaping.
 */
export function buildSubtitleFilter(srtPath, styleConfig) {
  const escapedPath = srtPath
    .replace(/\\/g, '/')
    .replace(/^([A-Za-z]):\//, '$1\\:/');

  // Since we now use .ass files, we don't need force_style overriding everything
  // as it destroys the ASS formatting. We just load it directly.
  return `subtitles='${escapedPath}'`;
}

export function buildSubtitleFilterSafe(srtPath, styleConfig) {
  try {
    const filter = buildSubtitleFilter(srtPath, styleConfig);
    if (/\\\\:/.test(filter)) {
      console.warn('[caption] Double-backslash detected in filter path — skipping captions:', filter);
      return null;
    }
    return filter;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[caption] buildSubtitleFilter threw:', err.message);
    return null;
  }
}

export { CAPTION_STYLE_IDS } from './captionStyles.js';



--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: backend\src\captionStyles.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------


/**
 * Caption preset registry for premium short-form captions.
 *
 * These presets drive the ASS subtitle styling, safe-area logic,
 * and emphasis behaviour used during export.
 */

function pickFont(language, options = {}) {
  const lang = (language || '').toLowerCase();
  const indic = ['hi', 'mr', 'gu', 'bn', 'ta', 'te', 'kn', 'ml', 'pa', 'ur', 'or', 'as', 'hinglish'];
  if (indic.some(c => lang.startsWith(c) || lang === c)) {
    return options.hindiFont || 'Nirmala UI';
  }
  return options.font || 'Montserrat ExtraBold';
}

function normalizeStyleKey(styleId = 'gaming') {
  return String(styleId || 'gaming').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
}

function toHexColor(value, fallback = '#FFFFFF') {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) return fallback;
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) return trimmed;
  return fallback;
}

export const CAPTION_STYLE_IDS = [
  'classic', 'tiktok', 'gaming', 'gaming-pro', 'streamer', 'meme', 'modern', 'cinematic', 'clean', 'minimal', 'karaoke', 'neon', 'viral'
];

export function buildStyleConfig(styleId, options = {}, language = 'en') {
  const font = pickFont(language, options);
  const scale = Number(options.fontScale || 1);
  const baseSize = Math.round((Number(options.fontSize || 24) || 24) * scale);
  const alignment = { bottom: '2', center: '5', top: '8' }[options.captionPosition || 'center'] || '5';

  const registry = {
    classic: {
      id: 'classic', fontFamily: font, fontWeight: 'normal', size: Math.max(18, baseSize),
      primaryColor: '#F8FAFC', outlineColor: '#0F172A', shadowColor: '#000000', backColor: '#00000000',
      bold: '0', outlineWidth: 3, shadowWidth: 2, alignment,

      bold: '1', outlineWidth: 7, shadowWidth: 5, alignment: '5',
      padding: 11, lineSpacing: 9, animation: 'bounce', safeArea: { marginV: 180, minY: 1310, anchor: 'bottom' },

  const styleKey = normalizeStyleKey(styleId);
  const preset = registry[styleKey] || registry.gaming;
  const merged = {
    ...preset,
    font: preset.fontFamily,
    fontFamily: preset.fontFamily,
    fontWeight: preset.fontWeight,
    size: Math.max(18, Math.round((preset.size || baseSize) * scale)),
    primary: toHexColor(preset.primaryColor, '#FFFFFF'),
    outline: toHexColor(preset.outlineColor, '#000000'),
    shadow: toHexColor(preset.shadowColor, '#000000'),
    back: toHexColor(preset.backColor, '#00000000'),
    outlineWidth: preset.outlineWidth || 4,
    shadowWidth: preset.shadowWidth || 2,
    padding: preset.padding || 8,
    lineSpacing: preset.lineSpacing || 8,
    animation: preset.animation || 'pop',
    safeArea: preset.safeArea || { marginV: 140, minY: 1360, anchor: 'bottom' },
    emphasis: { ...preset.emphasis, ...options.emphasis },
    wordsPerLine: preset.wordsPerLine || 1,
    emoji: preset.emoji !== undefined ? preset.emoji : true,
    wordPulse: preset.wordPulse !== undefined ? preset.wordPulse : true,
    uppercase: preset.uppercase !== undefined ? preset.uppercase : false,
    alignment: options.captionPosition ? { bottom: '2', center: '5', top: '8' }[options.captionPosition] || '5' : (preset.alignment || alignment),
    marginV: preset.safeArea?.marginV || 140,
    borderStyle: 1,
    backColour: preset.backColor || '#00000000',
    bold: preset.bold || '1',
  };

  return merged;
}

export function styleToFfmpegForceStyle(s) {
  const fontFamily = s.fontFamily || s.font || 'Montserrat ExtraBold';
  return `FontName=${fontFamily},FontSize=${s.size},PrimaryColour=${s.primary},OutlineColour=${s.outline},BackColour=${s.back},Bold=${s.bold},Outline=${s.outlineWidth || s.outlineW || 4},Shadow=${s.shadowWidth || s.shadow || 2},Alignment=${s.alignment || 5},MarginV=${s.marginV || 140}`;
}





--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: backend\src\chatPipeline.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------


/**
 * Future-ready chat explosion detection (Twitch / Kick / YouTube Live)
 * Stub pipeline — wire when chat logs are available per job.
 */

export const CHAT_SPAM_PATTERNS = {
  w: /^w+$/i,
  gg: /^gg+$/i,
  lol: /^lol+$/i,
  noway: /no\s*way|noway/i
};

export function analyzeChatWindow(messages = [], windowStart, windowEnd) {
  const inWindow = messages.filter(m => m.t >= windowStart && m.t <= windowEnd);
  if (!inWindow.length) return { spike: false, score: 0, dominant: null };

  const counts = { w: 0, gg: 0, lol: 0, noway: 0, total: inWindow.length };
  for (const m of inWindow) {
    const text = (m.text || '').trim();
    if (CHAT_SPAM_PATTERNS.w.test(text)) counts.w++;
    else if (CHAT_SPAM_PATTERNS.gg.test(text)) counts.gg++;
    else if (CHAT_SPAM_PATTERNS.lol.test(text)) counts.lol++;
    else if (CHAT_SPAM_PATTERNS.noway.test(text)) counts.noway++;
  }

  const rate = inWindow.length / Math.max(1, windowEnd - windowStart);
  const spike = rate > 2.5 || counts.w > 15 || counts.gg > 10;
  let dominant = null;
  let max = 0;
  for (const k of ['w', 'gg', 'lol', 'noway']) {
    if (counts[k] > max) { max = counts[k]; dominant = k; }
  }

  return {
    spike,
    score: Math.min(100, Math.round(rate * 12 + max * 2)),
    dominant,
    counts,
    platform: 'generic'
  };
}

export function createChatPipeline(job) {
  return {
    jobId: job.id,
    status: 'ready',
    platforms: ['twitch', 'kick', 'youtube_live'],
    ingest(messages) {
      job.chatMessages = job.chatMessages || [];
      job.chatMessages.push(...messages);
    },
    scoreWindow(start, end) {
      return analyzeChatWindow(job.chatMessages || [], start, end);
    }
  };
}





--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: backend\src\clipClassifier.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------


import { textInWindow } from './transcriptSchema.js';

const RULES = [
  { category: 'funny', re: /lol|lmao|funny|hilarious|हँस|मजाक|हा हा|rofl/i, weight: 1 },
  { category: 'rage', re: /rage|angry|wtf|mad|गुस्सा|पागल|scream/i, weight: 1 },
  { category: 'reaction', re: /react|omg|wow|no way|अरे|वाह|shocked/i, weight: 0.9 },
  { category: 'clutch', re: /clutch|1v\d|ace|last second|किल|बचा/i, weight: 1 },
  { category: 'win', re: /win|victory|champion|जीत|winner/i, weight: 0.9 },
  { category: 'educational', re: /learn|tip|how to|tutorial|सीख|टिप/i, weight: 0.8 },
  { category: 'most-viral', re: /.*/i, weight: 0.1 }
];

export const CLIP_CATEGORIES = [
  'funny', 'rage', 'reaction', 'clutch', 'win', 'educational', 'most-viral'
];

export function classifyClip(seg, transcriptSegments = [], virality = {}) {
  const text = `${seg.title || ''} ${seg.summary || ''} ${textInWindow(transcriptSegments, seg.start, seg.end)}`;
  let best = { category: 'reaction', score: 0 };

  for (const rule of RULES) {
    if (rule.category === 'most-viral') continue;
    if (rule.re.test(text)) {
      const s = rule.weight * 100 + (virality.finalScore || 0) * 0.1;
      if (s > best.score) best = { category: rule.category, score: s };
    }
  }

  if ((virality.finalScore || seg.score || 0) >= 85) {
    return { category: 'most-viral', ...virality };
  }
  return { category: best.category, categoryScore: best.score };
}

export function groupClipsByCategory(clips) {
  const groups = {};
  for (const c of CLIP_CATEGORIES) groups[c] = [];
  for (const clip of clips) {
    const cat = clip.category || 'reaction';
    if (groups[cat]) groups[cat].push(clip);
    else groups.reaction.push(clip);
  }
  return groups;
}

FILE: backend\src\effectsEngine.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------


/** Auto effects for stream highlights — punch zoom, shake, impact */

const EFFECT_BY_CATEGORY = {
  clutch: 'punch',
  win: 'impact',
  rage: 'shake',
  funny: 'punch',
  reaction: 'faceZoom'
};

export function pickEffectType(virality = {}, category = '') {
  if (category && EFFECT_BY_CATEGORY[category]) return EFFECT_BY_CATEGORY[category];
  const r = virality.reactionScore || 0;
  const s = virality.surpriseScore || 0;
  const e = virality.emotionScore || 0;
  if (r >= 82) return 'faceZoom';
  if (s >= 80) return 'impact';
  if (e >= 85) return 'punch';
  if (r >= 70) return 'motion';
  return null;
}

export function shouldApplyEffects(virality = {}, options = {}, category = '') {
  if (options.autoEffects === false) return false;
  const r = virality.reactionScore || 0;
  const e = virality.emotionScore || 0;
  const s = virality.surpriseScore || 0;
  if (pickEffectType(virality, category)) return true;
  return r >= 65 || e >= 72 || s >= 70;
}

/** Punch-in zoom for first ~0.8s (works on composed 9:16) */
function punchZoomFilter() {
  return `zoompan=z='if(lte(on,24),1+0.08*(on/24),1.08)':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':d=1:s=1080x1920:fps=30,scale=1080:1920,setsar=1`;
}

function impactZoomFilter() {
  return `zoompan=z='if(lte(on,18),1.12-0.12*(on/18),1)':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':d=1:s=1080x1920:fps=30,scale=1080:1920,setsar=1`;
}

function subtleShakeFilter() {
  return `crop=iw-2:ih-2:1+mod(n\\,4):1+mod(n+2\\,4),scale=1080:1920,setsar=1`;
}

function faceZoomFilter() {
  return `zoompan=z='min(zoom+0.002,1.1)':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/3-(ih/zoom/3)':d=1:s=1080x1920:fps=30,scale=1080:1920,setsar=1`;
}

function motionEmphasisFilter() {
  return `scale=iw*1.04:ih*1.04,crop=1080:1920:(iw-1080)/2:(ih-1920)/2,setsar=1`;
}

export function buildEffectsFilter(virality = {}, duration = 60, category = '', options = {}) {
  if (!shouldApplyEffects(virality, options, category)) return null;
  const type = pickEffectType(virality, category);
  switch (type) {
    case 'punch': return punchZoomFilter();
    case 'impact': return impactZoomFilter();
    case 'shake': return subtleShakeFilter();
    case 'faceZoom': return faceZoomFilter();
    case 'motion': return motionEmphasisFilter();
    default: return motionEmphasisFilter();
  }
}

/** Append effects after layout in filter_complex: [v] in → [vout] out */
export function chainEffectsInComplex(layoutFilter, virality, category, options = {}) {
  const fx = buildEffectsFilter(virality, 60, category, options);
  if (!fx) return layoutFilter;
  if (!layoutFilter.includes('[v]')) return layoutFilter;
  return layoutFilter.replace('[v]', `[vfx];[vfx]${fx}[v]`);
}

export function combineVideoFilters(...parts) {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(',');
}

/** Indian creator optimizations — hooks, titles, caption hints */

const HOOKS_HI = [
  'ये देखो क्या हुआ...',
  'कोई ये expect नहीं कर रहा था...',
  'आगे देखो क्या होता है...',
  'इसने सब कुछ बदल दिया...',
  'Wait for the end...'
];

const HOOKS_HINGLISH = [
  'Nobody expected this...',
  'Bhai ye dekh...',
  'Watch till the end...',
  'This changed everything...',
  'Full video mein ye best part hai...'
];

export function optimizeHooksForIndia(hooks = [], mode = {}) {
  if (!mode.enabled) return hooks;
  const pool = ['hi', 'mr', 'gu', 'bn', 'ta', 'te', 'kn', 'ml', 'pa', 'ur', 'or', 'as'].includes(mode.language) ? HOOKS_HI : HOOKS_HINGLISH;
  const out = [...hooks];
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(5, pool.length); i++) {
    if (!out[i] || out.length < 5) out[i] = out[i] || pool[i];
  }
  return out.slice(0, 5);
}

export function optimizeTitle(title, mode = {}) {
  if (!mode.enabled || !title) return title;
  if (mode.language === 'hi' && !/[\u0900-\u097F]/.test(title)) {
    return `${title} | Shorts`;
  }
  return title;
}

export function captionOptionsForIndia(options = {}, mode = {}) {
  if (!mode.enabled) return options;
  return {
    ...options,
    fontScale: (options.fontScale || 1) * 1.05,
    wordsPerLine: options.wordsPerLine || 4,
    hindiFont: 'Nirmala UI'
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\mediaAnalysis.js
